- **jj-concurrent**: Orchestrator + worker model for running many Claude agents concurrently on jj workspaces: the jj-delegate mechanism, the jj-workspace-worker agent, a snapshot hook (closes jj's crash-before-snapshot gap) and a guard hook (blocks raw mutating git, interactive jj, and destructive ops on the jj/git stores). Workflow-agnostic; useful in any jj repo. ([DannyGoodall/claude-code-jj](https://github.com/DannyGoodall/claude-code-jj/tree/HEAD/plugins/jj-concurrent) · 10 skills, 1 agent, 1 hook file)
- **jj-concurrent-linear**: Linear binding over jj-concurrent: at the orchestrator's reconcile point, maps each worker's structured JSON report to its Linear sub-issue (in-progress → done on a clean finish, blocker/conflict comments otherwise), threading umbrella + sub-issue IDs from dispatch keyed by workspace path. Separate, separately-enabled binding; enable only in Linear-tracked repos. Requires the jj-concurrent plugin and a configured Linear MCP server. No issue creation, no PR cross-linking; the worker stays Linear-agnostic. ([DannyGoodall/claude-code-jj](https://github.com/DannyGoodall/claude-code-jj/tree/HEAD/plugins/jj-concurrent-linear) · 3 skills)
- **jj-concurrent-openspec**: OpenSpec binding over jj-concurrent: /jj-openspec backgrounds an OpenSpec verb (apply / propose / new / ff) in its own jj workspace, mapping verb to shape (implementing vs authoring) and reconcile tail. Enable only in OpenSpec repos; requires the jj-concurrent plugin and the opsx skills. ([DannyGoodall/claude-code-jj](https://github.com/DannyGoodall/claude-code-jj/tree/HEAD/plugins/jj-concurrent-openspec) · 1 skill)
